### [161] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化支持向量机（structured SVM）在坐标式逐维argmax解码器下的Fisher一致性条件。作者证明"任务损失为度量且每个输出三元组存在公共测地点"这一已知必要但不充分，并给出4个输出的最小反例。随后完整分类了证明argmax一致性的正加权树度量（仅当树为路径时成立），发现分支树上的失败仅局限于边界分布，全支撑分布下所有树均保持argmax性质。文中还给出5输出全支撑反例（$K_{2,3}$为最小成员）、三维Hamming立方体的全支撑反例，并通过原始-对偶证书验证所有最优性论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化预测任务（如序列标注、多标签分类、结构化输出学习）的核心目标是学习一个能与任务损失对齐的决策函数。结构化SVM的Fisher一致性（即风险最小化收敛到Bayes最优解码）是保证方法可靠性的关键属性。已有理论指出若任务损失是度量且每个输出三元组有公共测地点，则损失是"可嵌入"的，这部分保证了预测一致性的必要条件。但该条件是否能保证坐标式argmax解码器的一致性，此前不清楚。本文的核心动机是检验这一必要条件的充分性，并精确刻画哪些度量结构能够保证结构化SVM的argmax一致性，从而为实际结构化预测系统的损失设计提供严格的理论边界。

Method: 本文采用严格的组合几何与凸优化方法。核心技术路线：(1) 构造最小反例——证明4个输出（unit star）存在完全最优分数向量，其所有argmax解码都是严格非Bayes的，并证明在满足公共测地条件的度量中4输出是极小的；(2) 完整分类正加权树度量——系统证明当且仅当输出空间树是路径（path）时argmax一致性成立；(3) 刻画失败区域——证明分支树的失败仅限于边界分布（边界支撑），全支撑分布下所有树仍保持argmax性质；(4) 全支撑反例——证明满足公共测地条件下需5个输出即可构造全支撑反例，$K_{2,3}$是无穷族$K_{m,n}$中最小的成员，并给出三维Hamming立方体的全支撑反例；(5) 所有最优性论断均给出精确的原始-对偶证书（primal-dual certificates），以在凸优化框架下严格验证优化性质。

Result: 本文以理论构造为主，无传统数据集实验，其"实验结果"体现为严格的数学定理与反例构造：通过Gurobi等优化工具求解结构化SVM的原始/对偶问题以获得精确证书，验证了以下结论：(1) 4输出单元星图存在反例，证明公共测地条件对坐标式argmax解码不充分；(2) 正加权树度量中argmax一致性成立当且仅当树为路径；(3) 分支树的反例仅存在于边界分布，全支撑分布下所有树均一致；(4) 5输出（$K_{2,3}$）为全支撑反例的最小规模，该反例可推广至任意$K_{m,n}$族；(5) 三维Hamming立方体存在全支撑反例。这些反例统一的启示是：在单纯形多面体（polyhedral）结构下，即使存在可保证校准链接（calibrated link）的嵌入，也无法保证预设的argmax链接在每个surrogate-risk最小化器上都成立，形成具体的decoder gap。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是精确刻画了结构化SVM中坐标式argmax解码的Fisher一致性的充分必要条件：在正加权树度量下等价于路径结构，并系统揭示了公共测地条件作为充分条件的失效模式及其最小反例规模（4输出边界情况、5输出全支撑情况）。其理论意义在于将一致性分析从"损失可嵌入性"推进到"解码器与嵌入的交互"，首次严格指出嵌入保证校准链接存在性并不足以替代对每个risk最小化器上argmax链接的事前验证。局限包括：结论集中于特定的多面体（polyhedral）设定与坐标式解码器，尚未推广到更一般的解码器族或非度量损失；对深度学习场景下神经网络surrogate的适用性有待进一步研究。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [162] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用"利润损失"直接评分氮肥施用量建议，而非传统预测精度指标。作者构建了一个基于892条真实产量响应曲线的测试基准，并扫描氮肥/谷物价格比覆盖所有价格情景，发现机器学习在预测上失败，但作为标准建议的"修正步骤"(correction)而非替代方案时，可削减四分之一的利润损失，并在第二试验站无需重训即削减43%损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冬小麦氮肥施用量在播种季前决定，而当时价格与天气未知。英国标准农业建议不随价格变化，但近年价格大幅波动使最盈利施用量变动达每公顷数十公斤。机器学习常被提议作为解决方案，但其通常以预测精度作为评判标准，而高精度预测并不自动意味着更盈利的建议。核心问题是：应以"放弃的利润"(forgone profit)直接给氮肥建议打分，而非仅看产量预测是否准确。该问题之所以重要，是因为其关乎真实经济效益而非学术指标。

Method: 方法核心有三点：(1) 构建测试基准——使用来自UK两个长期田间试验的892条测量的产量响应曲线，将氮/谷价格比在合理范围内扫描，覆盖全部价格情景；(2) 提出以"放弃的利润"作为氮肥建议的直接评分指标；(3) 关键创新是"简单修正步骤"(simple correction step)——在机器学习预测后施加的一个冻结(frozen)的修正算子，将标准建议与机器学习输出结合，作为混合方案(hybrid of standard advice plus damped correction)，用于去除偏差并削减罕见的大额损失。该方法刻意不追求更强的预测模型，而是将ML定位为对标准建议的利润评分修正而不是替代品。

Result: 在892条产量响应曲线上，机器学习作为预测器表现失败：没有任何模型能在农场容许范围内恢复最佳施氮量，且基准噪声(benchmark noise)表明任何模型都做不到。在正常价格下，所有模型在利润上均输给标准建议。但修正步骤带来增益：应用在模型之后可将利润损失削减四分之一；更强的模型和额外特征不带来增益。同一冻结修正方案在第二个试验站无需重新训练就削减43%的损失。该混合方案(标准建议加阻尼修正)可去除偏差并削减罕见大额损失。此外，同一价格扫描可对减排(emission cuts)进行定价，其成本与当前碳价格相当。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献是提出并验证了"以利润评分为视角"来评估农业氮肥建议的框架，得出反直觉结论：机器学习作为利润打分的修正步骤(依附于标准建议)才产生价值，而非作为独立预测器的替代品。其意义在于示范了一种可推广的评测原则——当最终目标是经济效益时应直接以经济指标评分，而非中间预测精度。局限包括：基于特定UK两试验站的产量曲线，跨地域、作物与气候的泛化性未验证；"农场容许范围"和修正算子的工程细节依赖基准设定；对价格与天气联合不确定性的处理较为简化。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [163] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO是一个面向惯性测量单元（IMU）的领域专用基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架同时解决传感异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异）和开放集识别（未知活动与标签词汇）两大挑战。第一阶段通过异构感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包含自适应池化分词、通道独立特征提取和基于自然语言传感器描述的上下文条件化；第二阶段通过同义词感知的软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，实现无逐数据集分类器的余弦相似度检索式开放集识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于IMU的人体活动识别（HAR）应用广泛，但现有方法缺乏能够跨不同受试者、设备和活动泛化的统一模型。训练此类模型面临两大核心难题：(1) 传感异构性——不同数据集在采样率、通道配置和传感器安置位置上的差异导致特征分布不一致；(2) 开放集泛化——模型需要处理训练中未见过的活动和标签词汇。这两个问题使传统的逐数据集训练方式无法产生可迁移的通用表示，亟需一个能统一处理异构传感输入且具备开放集能力的IMU基础模型。

Method: HALO采用两阶段训练框架。第一阶段进行异构感知的自监督预训练，关键技术包括：自适应池化分词（adaptive-pooling tokenization）统一不同采样率的输入；通道独立特征提取（channel-independent feature extraction）使各物理通道独立编码；上下文传感器条件化（contextualized sensor conditioning）将自然语言形式的传感器描述注入每个通道嵌入，使编码器感知通道的物理语义。第二阶段采用同义词感知的软对比学习（synonym-aware soft contrastive learning）将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用标签同义词间的语义关系构造软标签而非硬标签，从而通过余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需为每个数据集单独训练分类头。整体模型仅约3500万个可训练参数，远小于现有基础模型。

Result: 模型在10个公开HAR数据集上训练，在7个留出数据集上评估。HALO在全部8个聚合指标上超越五个SOTA基线，在基线匹配输入条件下仍在4个设置中的3个保持领先。在零样本开放集准确率（涵盖全部87个训练标签）上，HALO比最新的基础模型MOMENT（341.2M参数）提升13.7个百分点，而参数量仅为后者的约1/10。值得注意的是，在两个存在严重分布漂移的数据集上，包括HALO在内的所有模型零样本性能均完全崩溃，说明对极端域偏移的鲁棒性仍属未解难题。

Conclusion: HALO的核心贡献在于设计了领域专用的IMU基础模型训练范式，通过异构感知自监督预训练和语言对齐的开放集学习，以极少的参数量实现了更强的跨数据集迁移能力。其21世纪意义在于验证了"感知-语言对齐"范式可推广到IMU这种非视觉模态。局限性包括：在严重分布漂移场景下零样本能力完全失效，说明当前方法对极端域偏移缺乏鲁棒性保障；此外模型的开放集能力仍受限于训练标签词汇覆盖范围。真实世界视频演示见 https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [164] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于表格数据（tabular data）多头Transformer模型的可解释性重要性评分指标。通过对40个多样化的表格数据集进行实验，验证了基于注意力头重要性分数进行头部移除的鲁棒性：在72.5%的实验案例中，优先移除最低重要性分数的注意头时模型性能下降最缓，而优先移除最高重要性头则导致分类性能最大幅下降。该评分可用于提升Transformer架构的效率和冗余度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度Transformer模型在计算机视觉和自然语言处理领域已被广泛研究，但其在表格数据领域的应用和可解释性仍相对缺乏探索。计算密集且不透明的深度学习模型可以通过分析其数据变换方式被更好地理解和优化。多头注意力机制中不同头部的作用在不同领域中差异显著，理解表格数据中注意力头的重要性分布，有助于提供模型可解释性、识别冗余结构、提升计算效率，为表格数据下的Transformer架构提供优化依据。

Method: 本文的核心技术创新在于提出一种针对多头Transformer模型的注意力头重要性评分（head importance score）度量方法。该评分通过在测试阶段逐个移除（drop）注意力头并观测模型性能变化来量化每个头对分类预测的贡献：重要性最高与最低的头均通过其移除后对性能的影响来表征。实验采用系统性头部剪除策略——分别从最高到最低和从最低到最高的重要性顺序逐步移除头，对比性能曲线以验证评分有效性。该方法直接应用于表格数据上的多Transformer编码层（含6个注意力层）架构。

Result: 实验在40个不同表格数据集上进行，评估分类性能对注意力头移除的鲁棒性。主要发现包括：（1）在72.5%的实验案例中，按最低重要性分数顺序逐步移除注意力头时模型性能最不易下降（即模型对低重要性头的移除最鲁棒）；（2）相反，优先移除最高重要性头会带来最大幅度的分类性能下降；（3）对6个注意力层的头部重要性分数进行逐头分析发现，重要头并不集中在某层中，而是分散在各层之间，不存在一致的层级别趋势；（4）与图像、语言领域不同，表格数据中注意力头的重要性会随不同数据集（具有不同schema和特征空间）而显著变化。

Conclusion: 该工作是首次将重要性评分度量应用于表格数据多Transformer头可解释性的研究之一。其意义在于：证明了注意力头重要性分析可有效扩展到非图像、非文本的表格数据域，揭示了表格数据中头部重要性分布的自特性（跨数据集差异大、无统一层趋势），并为基于冗余削减的Transformer架构效率优化提供了可行路径。局限性在于：重要性评分的可靠性取决于粒度（逐头移除而非组合移除），且该方法在当前表格Transformer上的结论是否可泛化到更大规模架构和更复杂表格数据仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [165] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"电路冷凝"（Circuit Condensation）方法，通过交替进行低归属边的剪枝与低秩适配器训练，将大语言模型的因果行为压缩进更小的因果子图中。在4种行为和8个模型上的32种设置中，冷凝电路在30种设置下优于最强冻结基线，平均缩小8.1倍、最高达316倍。该方法在间接宾语识别任务上仅隔离出24个注意力头（其中17个有已知文档角色），相比之下冻结电路需要61个头，从而获得原论文已发表机制的一个充分子电路而非完整重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机理可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）的一种主流范式是通过"电路"（circuits）来理解模型行为——即承载该行为的组件与连接。然而，冻结式发现（frozen discovery）通常会返回成百上千条边，导致这些电路难以逐一检查、比较或穷举验证。这种规模使得人类研究者无法可靠地判断哪些组件真正必要，从而制约了对模型内部机制的理解与验证。

Method: 电路冷凝是一个迭代过程，每轮包含：(1) 剪除低归属（low-attribution）的边；(2) 训练一个低秩适配器（low-rank adapter），使剪枝后的模型通过保留的边匹配原始模型输出。仅当任务性能与通用能力（general capability）都保持时，才保留这次剪枝。该方法的关键新颖之处在于：剪枝后进行权重更新是驱动电路缩小的核心因素，而非单纯依赖搜索本身。

Result: 实验覆盖4种行为、8个模型，共32种设置。冷凝电路在30/32种设置中比最强冻结基线更小，平均缩小8.1倍，最高达316倍。对照实验表明：在无权重更新的情况下重复搜索，在29/32种设置中产生更大电路，说明权重更新而非搜索本身驱动了缩减。对19个电路的全子集测试发现其中11个无法进一步缩小，其余电路中有可移除的边；成对消融（pair ablations）揭示边之间存在依赖关系，说明其效应不能独立理解。在间接宾语识别（IOI）上，冷凝方法隔离出24个注意力头（17个有记录角色），而匹配的冻结电路需要61个头（36个无文档角色）。冷凝所得电路能追踪原始模型的下一个token分布并预测其错误行为。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一种新的电路压缩范式——通过后训练权重更新（而非单纯冻结搜索）来浓缩模型行为，从而显著缩小可验证的因果图规模。该方法在IOI上获得的足够子电路（sufficient sub-circuit）能复现原始机制的输入输出行为，即使它不是该机制的完整重建。局限包括：冷凝电路可能只捕获"充分"而非"必要"的机制，且成对消融表明边间存在复杂依赖，无法用独立归因来完全解释，电路的简化程度可能因行为和模型组合而异。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [166] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出潜在演化算子网络（LEON），将潜在世界动作模型（WAM）中的潜在状态转移实现为基于受控Koopman生成器视角的算子传播与加性强迫相结合的形式。与常用Transformer预测器不同，LEON在可学习的观测空间中通过上下文调制算子建模潜在演化，将上下文相关的转移变化组织在共享的演化算子结构周围，同时保留加性变化路径，从而提升了闭环性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来世界动作模型（WAMs）通过在潜在表示空间中预测任务相关场景状态的演化来增强机器人策略，避免全外观级生成。然而，现有WAMs普遍采用Transformer作为潜在转移预测器，其归纳结构以token交互为核心而非时间演化。作者指出，将潜在转移实现视为一个独立于预测表示和预测-策略耦合的建筑选择，并质疑Transformer的token交互结构是否能有效建模时间演化动力学。这一问题的核心在于：潜在转移实现方式对闭环控制性能的影响尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: LEON以受控Koopman生成器视角为理论基础，将潜在状态演化建模在学习的观测空间中。具体而言：(1) 通过上下文调制算子（context-modulated operator）进行算子传播，将上下文相关的转移变化组织在共享的演化算子结构周围；(2) 同时保留加性强迫（additive forcing）路径，用于建模Operator传播无法捕获的加性变化。LEON通过受控动力系统验证其演化特定的归纳偏置，以及算子传播与强迫的互补作用。论文在两种将潜在预测以不同方式集成到策略中的WAM公式上评测，包括完全转移替换和增量集成等场景。

Result: 论文首先在受控动力系统上验证了LEON的演化特定归纳偏置，确认算子传播与加性强迫的互补作用。随后在两种不同的WAM公式化上进行评测，这些公式化将潜在预测集成到策略中的方式不同。实验结果显示LEON在闭环性能（closed-loop performance）和鲁棒性上优于Transformer基线，并且在全转移替换（full transition replacement）条件下仍能保持有效性。这些结果表明转移实现方式在潜在WAM中是一个具有重要意义的结构性选择。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于：(1) 将潜在转移实现从预测表示和预测-策略耦合中分离出来，作为一个独立且重要的结构选择；(2) 提出基于受控Koopman生成器理论的LEON架构，通过算子传播与加性强迫的互补设计实现演化特定的归纳偏置；(3) 实证表明LEON在多种WAM集成方式下均提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。局限可能包括：实验规模的具体细节未在摘要中展开，Koopman算子框架对高维、强非线性潜在动力学的表达效率有待进一步验证，以及该方法在更多样的机器人操作任务上的泛化能力尚未充分展示。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [167] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-Spectrum，一个面向多模态多光谱谱图-结构解析任务的稀疏混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）框架。其核心创新是显式的模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份信息暴露给路由器而非仅依赖 token 内容表征，并设计了共享专家与交互专家以及异构专家容量，以提取模态独有信息和跨模态协同信息、抑制噪声干扰，在多模态不平衡场景下显著提升分子结构推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态光谱测量（如质谱、红外、核磁等）推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异质的信号。然而，直接拼接多光谱序列的常见范式会出现异常的性能退化，根源在于模态间差异显著导致的多模态不平衡（multimodal imbalance）。这种不平衡使得模型难以公平、有效地利用各模态信息，这是该论文要解决的核心问题。该问题在化学信息学、药物发现等依赖光谱结构解析的实际应用中具有重要价值。

Method: MM-Spectrum 的主要技术贡献包括三方面：1) 显式模态感知路由机制：在稀疏 MoE 路由器中输入中除 token 内容表征外，还显式注入光谱身份（modality identity）信息，使路由决策能感知当前信息来源，从而更好匹配多光谱不平衡下的信息特征；2) 共享专家与交互专家设计：路由结构中同时包含共享专家（提取各模态通用的信息）和交互专家（建模跨模态协同特征），用于提取模态独有信息与跨模态联合信息；3) 异构专家容量：不同专家采用差异化容量，以适配各模态噪声特性，从而抑制噪声诱导的干扰，提升鲁棒性。

Result: 论文在分子结构解析任务上进行了全模态、双模态以及缺失模态（missing-modality）三种设置下的实验评估。MM-Spectrum 在所有设置下均取得一致且显著的性能提升。此外，消融实验（ablation studies）验证了模态感知路由、共享/交互专家及异构容量各自的有效性，可解释性分析（interpretability analyses）进一步揭示了各组件的作用机制。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum 是针对多模态多光谱谱图-结构解析的首个面向模态不平衡问题的稀疏 MoE 方法，其显式模态身份路由与共享/交互专家设计为缓解多模态异质性和不平衡提供了有效方案。该方法在完整模态、双模态及缺失模态设置下均表现稳定且提升显著。主要局限性在于论文聚焦于化学光谱解析领域，其模态感知路由与专家设计在事件相机视觉、UAV 感知等其他多模态任务上的通用性与可迁移性尚待验证，且大规模分子结构库上的可扩展性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [168] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumBoostNet，一种用于超声心动图（心脏超声）视图识别的混合经典-量子架构。该模型由一个经典骨干网络和两个输出头组成：一个经典头和一个由10量子比特参数化量子电路实现的量子头。训练分两阶段进行，通过监测损失动态的混合参数实现两个头之间的自适应切换。实验表明，尽管可模拟的量子比特数量有限，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务上持续超越最先进的经典及混合经典-量子模型，并在标准图像分类基准上表现优异，且对噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声（超声心动图）的视图/角度准确识别是心血管影像学的关键环节，直接影响解剖结构判读、测量可靠性和临床误差的降低。尽管计算机视觉模型在标准基准上表现良好，但由于医学影像数据存在高水平噪声，现有最先进模型在专门医学影像任务上往往效果欠佳。因此，需要探索能够处理高噪声医学数据、并在专用任务上实现性能突破的新型架构，特别是利用量子计算特性来增强经典模型的表达能力。

Method: QuantumBoostNet采用混合经典-量子架构：以经典骨干网络为主体，配备两个输出头——一个为经典头，另一个为量子头。量子头实现为参数化的10量子比特量子电路（parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit）。训练采用两阶段策略，通过一个混合参数（mixing parameter）监测损失动态，实现对两个输出头之间的自适应过渡（adaptive transition）。该机制允许模型在训练过程中根据损失态势动态决定由经典头还是量子头主导决策，从而平衡经典特征提取能力与量子电路的高维表示能力。

Result: 实验设置包括心脏超声视图识别任务以及标准图像分类基准。主要实验结果表明：（1）尽管受限于可模拟量子比特的数量，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别上持续优于最先进的经典模型和混合经典-量子模型，相对最佳竞争者取得性能提升；（2）在已确立的图像分类基准上，QuantumBoostNet同样展现出优越性能；（3）该模型对噪声表现出鲁棒性。论文以相对改进（relative improvement）作为关键比较指标。

Conclusion: QuantumBoostNet的核心贡献在于提出了一种经典骨干与量子头相结合的双分支混合架构，并设计了基于损失动态的自适应头切换训练策略，有效应对医学影像中高噪声数据的挑战，在心脏超声视图识别这一专用医疗影像任务上超越现有SOTA。其意义在于验证了混合经典-量子模型在专用医学影像应用中的潜力。局限性主要包括：受限于当前可在经典设备上模拟的量子比特数量（仅10 qubit），量子电路的规模与表示能力受限；研究尚未涉及真实量子硬件部署、更大数据规模或更多临床任务泛化性的讨论。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对块式草拟（block drafting）推测解码中"拒绝率来源混淆"的问题，提出用**信息下限（information floor）**将两项拒绝损失分离：一是块内缺失的路径信息（conditioning order不足），二是生成模型对可观测信息的建模不完善（模型缺口）。作者在四个域、四个开放权重目标模型和一个前沿API目标上，对多个草拟器（DFlash、DSpark等）进行估计，得出三个关键发现：全并行草拟在末位槽的拒绝下限达0.286（Qwen3-4B），将最佳提案的每槽接受率限制在71%；只要实现一个token即可消除86%–100%的下限（该局部性也被独立互信息分析验证）；现有草拟器仍远高于其信息下限——末位槽的模型缺口占DFlash拒绝的43%–64%、占DSpark oracle条件拒绝的85%–92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块式草拟（block drafters）在一次前向传播中同时提出多个token（在更早目标token尚未实现之前就给出），其拒绝率混合了两种本质上不同的损失来源：(1) 块内草拟缺少真实前序token路径信息（within-block path information）；(2) 模型对可观测信息的建模不完美。由于"被接受的长度"无法区分这两种来源，导致无法准确评估草拟器与提案模型本身的真实质量。该问题对于推测解码（speculative decoding）的部署优化和研究评估具有重要性，因为直接影响吞吐量与延迟收益的判断。

Method: 核心思想是引入一个**信息下限（information floor）**：在给定指定条件顺序（conditioning order）下，理论上可实现的最小期望拒绝率（即仅由缺失的块内路径信息导致的拒绝）。总拒绝中高于该信息下限的部分被定义为**模型缺口（model gap）**，反映模型对可观测信息的建模不完美程度。通过从目标模型的rollout中同时估计信息下限与模型缺口，作者将短程条件作用的价值与提案模型质量解耦。该方法具有通用性：可应用于不同草拟器、不同目标模型以及不同数据域，并辅以独立的互信息分析验证局部性结论。

Result: 实验覆盖四个数据域、四个开放权重目标模型（含Qwen3-4B）以及一个前沿API目标模型，评估DFlash、DSpark等块式草拟器。三个主要发现：(1) 全并行（all-parallel）草拟在末位槽（final slot）的信息下限达0.286（Qwen3-4B），将最佳提案方法的每槽接受率限制在约71%；(2) 仅实现一个已realised的token即可移除86%–100%的该下限，说明短程条件作用带来绝大部分信息收益，此局部性由独立互信息分析交叉验证；(3) 现有草拟器仍远高于其信息下限——末位槽的模型缺口占DFlash拒绝的43%–64%，占DSpark oracle条件化拒绝的85%–92%，说明提案模型质量仍为主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是建立了区分"短程条件作用价值"与"提案模型质量"的量化框架（信息下限 vs 模型缺口），为块式推测解码提供了可操作的分析工具。结论表明：当前草拟器的主要改进空间来自削减模型缺口而非增加更多条件token，且仅需一个已realised token就能捕获绝大多数信息增益。其意义在于指导草拟器设计与条件化策略优化（如两阶段/顺序草拟）。局限在于结论基于特定目标模型与草拟器集合，信息下限估计依赖rollout采样，对长序列或更大规模模型的泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [170] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了进化策略（ES）作为LLM推理后训练范式的优化行为与机制，理论及实证表明ES相较于GRPO能够带来更广泛的推理覆盖（更高的Pass@K），同时保持Pass@1不损失；提出了顺序GRPO-ES联合训练策略，并揭示了ES的参数更新具有功能稀疏性——整体参数漂移大但功能性变化集中在少数大幅更新上，不会必然引发灾难性遗忘，且ES所需种群大小随模型规模增大而减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略（ES）虽已被证明是一种内存高效（memory-efficient）的LLM推理后训练范式，但其优化行为长期缺乏深入研究，导致难以界定其相对于主流后训练范式（如GRPO）的优势范围。该问题的重要性在于：若不从机制层面理解ES的优化动力学，就无法科学定位其适用场景，也无法指导超参数设计。论文旨在填补这一空白，通过理论分析和系统实验回答ES到底在何种条件下优于GRPO、其内在机制为何，从而推动推理后训练范式选择的理性化。

Method: 论文采用理论分析与实证结合的方法。理论上，作者证明了验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon散度（verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity）对ES种群的高Pass@K表现有帮助，从信息论角度解释了种群多样性为何关键。实证层面：（1）对比ES与GRPO的动力学差异，指出GRPO存在熵坍缩（entropy collapse）现象而ES不会，从而ES在提升Pass@1的同时获得更高Pass@K；（2）提出顺序GRPO-ES训练策略，融合GRPO在Pass@1的优势与ES在Pass@K的优势；（3）通过分析参数更新分布，发现ES虽导致整体模型参数大幅漂移，但其任务性能增益仅来自少数大幅更新的稀疏子集，揭示"大参数移动未必意味着广泛功能性变化"的功能稀疏性特征；（4）通过held-out评估验证该稀疏性不会必然导致灾难性遗忘；（5）研究超参数设计，发现更大的LLM需要更小的ES种群规模。

Result: 论文的关键实验发现包括：（1）ES在Pass@K上系统性优于GRPO，而两者在Pass@1上可比，ES通过更广泛的推理覆盖更好地挖掘预训练LLM的推理能力；（2）GRPO表现出熵坍缩，而ES维持更高种群多样性，这与理论上的JS散度分析一致；（3）顺序GRPO-ES训练可实现Pass@1与Pass@K的双重提升；（4）ES的功能稀疏性：尽管全模型参数漂移显著，性能增益仅归因于少数大幅更新，held-out评估表明此现象不必然导致灾难性遗忘；（5）ES种群规模与模型规模呈负相关，即更大LLM需要更小种群。这些结果将ES定位为一种区别于GRPO的独立推理后训练范式，而非效率较低的内存节省替代方案。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是从理论（JS散度视角）与实证（ES动力学、功能稀疏性、超参数设计）两个层面系统阐明了ES作为LLM推理后训练范式的优势机制，证明其并非GRPO的低效替代，而是一个具有独特优势（更广推理覆盖、更高Pass@K）的独立范式，并给出实用的GRPO-ES联合训练策略。局限性在于：论文主要考察推理任务上的表现，功能稀疏性与遗忘现象的普适性尚需在更多下游任务上验证；超参数规律（种群大小随模型规模减小）的泛化边界也待进一步探索。虽不直接涉及事件相机或无人机感知，但其关于推理覆盖、熵/多样性调控及内存高效后训练的洞见，对事件流下游任务中面向高效实时推理的小模型精调策略具有潜在借鉴价值。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>

### [171] [Bug Localization from Bug Reports: A Multi-Objective Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27089)
*Waleed Ahmad,Mehtab Kiran Suddle,Maryam Bashir*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种类级别的多目标搜索式自动缺陷定位系统，采用SPEA-2进化算法，通过最大化bug报告与源代码的相似度、同时最小化建议的缺陷文件数量，来对可疑缺陷类进行排序。在6个开源Java项目（超过22,000个bug报告）上验证，其精确率和召回率优于NSGA-II、MOEA/D及单目标基线方法，并在Kotlin编写的工业Android项目上证明跨语言适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷定位是大型软件系统中劳动密集且耗时的任务，开发者需要重复性步骤来定位异常行为对应的缺陷文件。现有方法的不足包括：(1) 多数方法局限于单一目标优化，且许多受限于特定编程语言；(2) 仅依赖源码与bug报告之间的词法相似度往往不够，因为bug描述是自然语言，存在语义鸿沟。因此需要一个能同时优化多个目标（相似度最大化、推荐文件数最小化）的自动化类级定位系统。

Method: 该研究将缺陷定位建模为多目标优化问题：(1) 最大化bug报告与候选类/文件的相似度；(2) 最小化建议的缺陷文件数量。采用SPEA-2（Strength Pareto Evolutionary Algorithm 2）作为进化优化算法，对候选缺陷类进行排序和推荐。相似的算法NSGA-II和MOEA/D被用作对比基线。系统工作在类级别粒度，能够输出排序后的可疑buggy类/文件列表，并在Java之外验证了其对Kotlin语言的迁移能力。

Result: 在6个开源Java项目（含超过22,000个bug报告）上进行评估，并与NSGA-II、MOEA/D两种广泛使用的多目标算法及单目标基线方法对比。结果表明：(1) SPEA-2在精确率和召回率上均优于多目标与单目标基线方法；(2) 推荐系统能在Top-10内正确识别88.5%的bug报告对应缺陷类/文件，Top-20内达到94%；(3) 在Kotlin编写的工业级Android项目上进一步验证，证明了其在跨编程语言场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一个类级别的、基于多目标进化搜索（SPEA-2）的缺陷定位推荐系统，有效解决了传统单目标方法的局限和自然语言与代码间的语义鸿沟问题，并通过跨语言（Java→Kotlin）验证展示其通用性。局限在于：并未引入深层语义/句法建模（仍以相似度+多目标优化为主），对语义鸿沟的解决依赖优化策略而非表示学习；验证集中于Java/Kotlin，对其他语言和真实大型工业系统的可扩展性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Bug localization is a labor-intensive task, particularly in large software systems. When abnormal behavior occurs, developers must perform repetitive and time-consuming steps to identify faulty files. Previous studies have mainly focused on single-objective localization methods, many of which are limited to specific programming languages. In addition, relying solely on lexical similarity between source code and bug reports is often insufficient due to the natural language nature of bug descriptions. In this study, we propose a class-level automated multi-objective search-based system to identify and rank potentially buggy classes from bug reports. The main objective is to maximize similarity while minimizing the number of suggested faulty files. The evolutionary optimization algorithm SPEA-2 was applied to six open-source Java projects comprising more than 22,000 bug reports. The proposed approach was evaluated against two widely used algorithms, NSGA-II and MOEA/D. Results indicate that SPEA-2 achieved higher precision and recall than both multi-objective and single-objective baseline methods. The proposed recommender system successfully identified buggy classes or files for 88.5\% of bug reports within the top 10 recommendations and 94\% within the top 20. The effectiveness of the model was further validated on an industrial Android project written in Kotlin, demonstrating its adaptability across programming languages.

</details>


### [172] [ANTShapes Benchmarking Datasets for Event-Based Neuromorphic Object Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27150)
*M. Middleton,H. Kayan,B. Sen Bhattacharya,T. Ali,E. Baikas,M. Vousden,C. Perera,O. Rhodes,E. Gheorghiu,M. A. Trefzer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了使用ANTShapes仿真工具生成的四个新型事件视觉分类数据集（难度各异），并与四种常用脉冲数据集（N-MNIST、CIFAR10-DVS、DVSGesture、POKER-DVS）进行基准对比。作者采用卷积脉冲神经网络（SNN）进行分类实验，同时提供了四个细节丰富的数据集供后续研究，并验证了ANTShapes工具的仿真输出适用于事件视觉分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧式相机的对象分类方案在功耗、体积和重量上难以满足极端边缘部署或隐蔽感知场景的需求，同时云/离设备计算存在数据安全风险、传输延迟及对持续网络连接的依赖。事件视觉与神经形态设备（SNN）有望解决上述问题，但该领域研究受限于高质量事件视觉数据集的匮乏。因此，本文旨在通过仿真工具生成更多样的数据集并验证其适用性，以推动基于事件的物体分类研究。

Method: 作者使用已提出的ANTShapes仿真工具创建了四个难度递增的新型事件视觉分类数据集（覆盖不同复杂度、形态和目标数量）。为验证数据质量，作者采用卷积SNN架构作为统一分类器，对所生成的数据集以及N-MNIST、CIFAR10-DVS、DVSGesture和POKER-DVS四个公认的脉冲数据基准进行端到端分类实验。方法核心在于：1）利用仿真工具批量生成带标签的事件流数据；2）用同一基准CNN-SNN模型在不同数据集上进行公平对比，以评估新数据集的可学习性与合理性。

Result: 研究设置了四组不同难度的新数据集，并对照四种现有脉冲数据集进行基准测试。实验采用卷积SNN完成事件流对象分类，考察各数据集的分类性能。结果表明所生成的数据集具有丰富的空间-时间细节，并能在SNN分类框架下取得合理的性能表现，从而验证了ANTShapes仿真工具生成的数据可用于事件视觉研究。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：（1）发布四个细节丰富、难度梯度设计的新型事件视觉数据集，填补了该领域高质量标注数据不足的空白；（2）通过统一CNN-SNN基准验证了ANTShapes工具的仿真数据输出适用于事件分类任务。潜在局限包括：数据集完全来自仿真，缺乏真实场景的域适配验证；分类任务限于特定目标形态，泛化性有待进一步测试。

Abstract: Object classification in event-based computer vision is a task that is attracting considerable research attention. Event-based object classification is a fundamental task in the fields of security and applied computer vision, which typically use synchronous frame-based cameras and computing pipelines for operation. This approach has several practical flaws. The size, weight and power consumption of the device could prohibit deployment at the extreme edge or in covert sensing environments. Besides this, there are security concerns inherent in cloud-based or other off-device computation approaches due to the requirement of sending and receiving potentially sensitive data. Furthermore, this transmission of data introduces latency and requires consistent connectivity to the cloud infrastructure to function.
  The use of Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) hosted on neuromorphic devices attempts to solve several issues present in this conventional approach. Research into event-based object classification methods are hindered by the lack of high-quality vision datasets to use. To this end, the ANTShapes simulation tool has been previously proposed to create and label event-based vision datasets. In this paper, four novel datasets of varying difficulties are created using the tool and are benchmarked against existing spiking datasets commonly used for event-based vision research (N-MNIST, CIFAR10-DVS, DVSGesture and POKER-DVS). Classification is performed using a convolutional SNN. This work simultaneously provides four datasets with rich details for future experiments to use and validates the output of the ANTShapes dataset simulation tool as being suitable for its purpose.

</details>

### [173] [WALL-SS: Scaling Long-horizon World Models via Next-Scale Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26239)
*Maeve Zhang,Rain Sun,Xiang Wang,Cyril Zhang,Shalfun Li,Meng Cao,Howard Lu,Ethan Chen,Harry Jhou,KZ Zheng,Lights Shi,Regis Cheng,Lorenzin,Robert Wang,Victor Yao,Gody Li,Elise Mon,Yohann Tang,Ryan Yu,PS Zhang,Vincent Chen,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WALL-SS是一种生成式世界模型，通过"尺度级自回归缩放"（Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling）来生成视觉未来帧，实现行动可控的长时程机器人仿真。它将具身轨迹表示为观测和动作交替的因果序列，用由粗到细的方式逐级生成未来观测，并结合行动条件化的下一尺度预测、尺度压缩的长时程记忆以及策略内对齐三个互补机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型为机器人提供交互作用下世界如何演化的预测模型，在仿真、规划、策略评估和机器人学习中潜力巨大。然而，现有方法大多局限于片段级（clip-level）的未来预测，缺乏一个统一生成式表述来：(1) 将动作与后果显式关联；(2) 支持灵活的预测时域和连续交互；(3) 支持奖励驱动的优化。该论文旨在解决长时程、行动可控的视觉仿真这一关键问题。

Method: WALL-SS将具身轨迹建模为"时间交错观测与动作"的因果序列，使行动依赖的状态迁移显式化，天然支持变长生成、通过可复用因果状态进行流式扩展，以及通过序列概率直接优化。为使该表述在长时程有效，采用由粗到细（coarse-to-fine）方式逐级生成各未来观测，并在同一层次结构内开发三个互补组件：(1) 行动条件化的下一尺度预测（Action-conditioned next-scale prediction）——注入尺度对齐的动作表示以增强动作-未来耦合并建模成功与失败行为；(2) 尺度压缩的长时程记忆（Scale-compressed long-horizon memory）——以高分辨率保留近期交互、压缩远距离观测与动作，并用尺度级dream forcing增强对自生成上下文的鲁棒性；(3) 策略内对齐（On-policy alignment）——以动作跟随和长期一致性奖励优化自回归视觉动态，同时保持预训练视觉分布。

Result: 实验表明，WALL-SS在动作跟随（action following）和轨迹精度（trajectory accuracy）上均有提升，能够支持在有界记忆条件下一致的分钟级长流式rollout，并且策略内对齐始终有助于减少动作漂移（action drift）和长时程不一致性（long-horizon inconsistency）。论文未在摘要中给出具体数据集名称和定量数值，仅报告了定性结论和相对提升。

Conclusion: WALL-SS的主要贡献在于提出一个统一、可行动的生成式世界模型表述，将动作-后果关联、灵活时域、连续交互与奖励驱动优化整合到一个由粗到细的自回归框架中，并通过三个互补组件解决长时程生成的耦合性、记忆压缩和一致性挑战。可能的局限包括：摘要未详述具体评测基准与定量指标，难以横向对比；长时程分钟级生成的可行性与大规模真实机器人场景的泛化能力仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative world models provide robots with predictive models of how the world evolves under interaction, with growing potential for simulation, planning, policy evaluation, and robot learning. Beyond clip-level future prediction, a unified generative formulation should relate actions to consequences, support flexible horizons and continuous interaction, and enable reward-driven optimization. We introduce WALL-SS, a world model that generates visual futures through Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling, enabling action-controllable and long-horizon robotic simulation. WALL-SS represents embodied trajectories as causal sequences of temporally interleaved observations and actions, making action-dependent state transitions explicit while naturally supporting variable-length generation, streaming extension through reusable causal states, and direct optimization through sequence probabilities. To make this formulation effective over long horizons, we generate each future observation in a coarse-to-fine manner and develop three complementary components within the same hierarchy. Action-conditioned next-scale prediction injects scale-aligned action representations to improve action-future coupling and model both successful and failed behaviors. Scale-compressed long-horizon memory retains recent interactions at fine resolution while compressing distant observations and actions, with scale-wise dream forcing enhancing robustness to self-generated context. Finally, on-policy alignment optimizes autoregressive visual dynamics with action-following and long-term consistency rewards while preserving the pretrained visual distribution. Experiments show that WALL-SS improves action following and trajectory accuracy, supports coherent minute-long streaming rollout under bounded memory, and consistently benefits from on-policy alignment in reducing action drift and long-horizon inconsistency.

</details>


### [174] [Constraint-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Static Shape Estimation of Co-Manipulative Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26273)
*Rana Danesh,Pari Qarehdaghi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束感知的物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于腱驱动连续体机器人（CCR）的静态形状估计。该方法将投影静态平衡残差和构型级几何残差嵌入网络损失，联合满足静力平衡与闭环几何约束，相较于纯数据驱动的ANN和迭代非线性求解器，在精度、物理一致性和推理速度上均有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同操纵连续体机器人（CCR）在抓取柔性物体时，连续体臂与被操纵物体共同构成一个闭环系统，其形状估计必须同时满足静态平衡方程和几何闭环约束，这本质上是一个强耦合的力学-几何问题。传统迭代非线性求解器（如17.97秒）虽精度高但实时性差，而纯数据驱动的神经网络在训练数据有限且有噪声时容易产生物理不一致的估计结果（偏离真实静力平衡和不满足闭环条件）。因此需要一种既能嵌入物理约束、又能实现快速推理的求解框架。

Method: 该方法基于几何变分应变（geometric variable strain）公式对腱驱动CCR建模，核心创新在于将两类物理约束以残差形式嵌入PINN的训练损失中：(1) 投影静态平衡残差（projected static equilibrium residual），用于强化控制微分方程（静力平衡）的满足；(2) 构型级几何残差（configuration-level geometric residual），用于强化闭环几何关系（open/closed-loop约束）。网络在推理时无需迭代求解，直接前向输出满足物理一致性的形状估计。此外，方法还包含实验数据微调环节，使网络适应真实系统的不确定性。

Result: 仿真实验中，在140个样本和50%标签噪声下，相较于纯数据驱动ANN，PINN将相对构型误差降低67.88%、平衡残差降低67.35%、闭环残差降低88.06%。使用完整数据集时，PINN达到0.1597%的相对构型误差，推理时间仅0.1773 ms，而迭代非线性求解器需17.97秒。实验微调后，标记点RMSE从2.657 mm降至0.497 mm，R²从-0.788提升至0.937，验证了在真实系统中的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是提出了一种物理约束内嵌的PINN框架，将闭环连续体系统的静力平衡与几何一致性同时纳入学习目标，实现了毫秒级、物理一致且高精度的静态形状估计。其意义在于为闭链连续体机器人提供了一种替代传统迭代求解器的实时估计方案。可能的局限性包括：模型依赖几何变分应变建模的准确性，对系统参数（如腱力、材料刚度）变化的鲁棒性尚需进一步验证，且多任务或多臂协同等复杂场景的扩展性有待探索。

Abstract: Static shape estimation of co-manipulative continuum robots (CCRs) is challenging because the continuum arms and manipulated flexible object form a closed chain that must satisfy both static equilibrium and geometric loop-closure constraints. This paper presents a constraint-aware physics-informed neural network (PINN) for static shape estimation of a tendon-driven CCR modeled using the geometric variable strain formulation. The proposed method incorporates a projected static equilibrium residual and a configuration-level geometric residual to enforce the governing mechanics and closed-chain geometry. In simulation, the PINN is compared with a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN) under limited and noisy training data. With 140 samples and 50% label noise, the PINN reduces the relative configuration error, equilibrium residual, and closed-chain residual by 67.88%, 67.35%, and 88.06%, respectively. Using the full dataset, the PINN achieves 0.1597% relative configuration error with an inference time of 0.1773 ms, compared with 17.97 s for an iterative nonlinear solver. Experimental fine-tuning reduces the marker RMSE from 2.657 mm to 0.497 mm and increases R2 from -0.788 to 0.937. These results demonstrate accurate, physically consistent, and computationally efficient static shape estimation of closed-chain CCRs.

</details>


### [175] [Dispersive Forward Tree Search for Optimal Control: Coverage, Complexity, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26314)
*Shashank A. Deshpande,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前向传播的运动规划器——离散前向树搜索算法（Dispersive Forward Tree search, DFT*），面向微分平坦非线性系统，具备确定性有限样本近最优性保证。其核心创新包括：构造局部离散控制命令集（dispersive command sets）以构建前向树、理论证明树规模指数下界、并提出成本条件主导剪枝过程使得树规模在问题时间域上达到多项式级别。该算法以广度优先方式展开，天然映射到并行硬件，已在单轮驱动车、拖车和四旋翼平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于转向（steering）的规划器需要求解状态对状态的两点边值问题（BVP），这对于非线性平台往往是不可行的。前向传播方法虽回避了转向需求，但其有限样本行为缺乏理论刻画，且实际实现性能欠佳。本文旨在填补这一空白：在微分平坦非线性系统的大类框架下，建立前向树搜索的确定性有限样本近最优性保证，并解决理论与实用实现之间的鸿沟，使其能在嵌入式平台上完成实时规划。

Method: 关键技术贡献包括三方面。（1）理论层面：证明在微分平坦非线性系统中，由局部离散控制命令组成的『前向树』在达到认证树规模时包含近最优轨迹；系统化解耦为通过集中证明广覆盖问题需要指数规模树且代价（成本）无关。（2）提出一种通用机制来为控制仿射系统构造离散命令集，这是实现理论所规定搜索算法的必要条件。（3）提出成本条件主导剪枝（cost-conditioned dominance pruning）过程，在不损失近最优性的前提下将树规模从指数降低到与问题时间域呈多项式关系。最终算法DFT*以广度优先方式展开前向树，天然适合并行硬件映射。作者还针对单轮驱动车、拖车车和四旋翼设计了高效的离散采样器。

Result: 作者在单轮驱动车、拖车和四旋翼三种平台上设计了高效的离散采样器，并评估了具有挑战性的规划任务。DFT*在嵌入式级处理器上以相当的求解时间下，始终提供具有竞争力、往往显著更优的解质量，且随着并行计算规模扩大而进一步加速。DFT*还被实现在滚动时域（receding-horizon）闭环中，展示了在动态环境中以嵌入式级计算预算进行实时规划的能力。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于将前向传播式动力规划器从『实践有效但缺乏理论保证』提升为具有确定性有限样本近最优性保证的算法，同时通过成本条件剪枝解决了理论所需指数树规模与实际计算可行性的矛盾。该工作的意义在于为非线性运动规划提供了无需转向BVP求解、可并行、具备可证明保证的通用框架。潜在局限包括：依赖微分平坦这一系统类假设，控制仿射构造机制的限制，以及仿真实验范围可能未覆盖真实硬件噪声和扰动情形。

Abstract: Steering-based planners require solutions to state-to-state boundary value problems, which can be inaccessible for nonlinear platforms. Forward propagation evades the steering requirement, but the finite-sample behavior of the associated planners remains uncharacterized and their implementations underperform in practice. This paper develops a propagation-based kinodynamic planner with deterministic finite-sample near-optimality guarantees. We work within the large class of differentially flat nonlinear systems and show that a forward tree of locally dispersive control commands contains a near-optimal trajectory at a certified tree size. We provide a general mechanism to construct dispersive command sets for control-affine systems, which are necessary to implement the search algorithm prescribed by the theory. We show that covering the certified trajectory class irrespective of cost provably demands a tree exponentially sized in the problem horizon, and present a cost-conditioned dominance pruning procedure that retains near-optimality at a tree size polynomial in the horizon. We implement the resulting search algorithm, Dispersive Forward Tree search (DFT*), as breadth-first expansion of the forward tree, which maps naturally onto parallel hardware. We design efficient dispersive samplers for the unicycle, the trailer car, and the quadrotor and evaluate challenging planning tasks for these platforms. DFT* delivers consistently competitive and often substantially better solution quality than state-of-the-art kinodynamic planners at comparable solution times on embedded-tier processors, accelerating further as parallel compute is scaled. We also implement DFT* in a receding-horizon loop to demonstrate real-time planning in dynamic environments at embedded-tier compute budgets.

</details>


### [176] [Cross-Platform Benchmark of Neural 3D Reconstruction for Autonomous Laboratory Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26383)
*Yongho Kim,Mengjiao Han,Victor Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Michael E. Papka,Nicola Ferrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对神经3D重建方法在机器人实验任务中的实时可用性进行了系统性计算平台基准测试，对比了NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）在从单板计算机到服务器级节点的GPU平台上的训练与渲染性能，并将Meta的SAM3D单图重建置于同一坐标系下评估其延迟与保真度差距。研究发现3DGS渲染质量优于NeRF但GPU开销更大，机载计算无法支撑交互速率的逐场景优化，而SAM3D可在数秒内生成合理几何但细节失配会影响下游操作，据此提出层级化管线设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自主机器人依赖3D重建管线将原始相机流转化为可操作的对象表征，且必须在物理控制回路的延迟预算内完成。尽管神经3D重建方法（NeRF、3DGS）已展示高质量视图合成能力，但其在实际实验室机器人所运行的计算平台上的实时可行性缺乏系统性的量化刻画——这是该工作要填补的关键空白。该问题的意义在于：实时重建能力直接决定机器人能否在闭环控制中可靠感知和操作物体，而现有文献多聚焦于方法精度提升，忽视了计算平台约束这一实际部署瓶颈。

Method: 本文采用系统化基准测试方法学：(1) 构建跨层级的GPU计算平台谱系，覆盖从单板计算机（single-board computers）到服务器级（server-class）节点的多种算力设备；(2) 在统一轴上对比NeRF与3D Gaussian Splatting的训练和渲染（training and rendering）性能，度量其质量-延迟-算力开销权衡；(3) 将Meta的SAM3D单图像重建（single-image reconstruction）纳入同一评估框架，量化其与前两者的延迟和保真度差距；(4) 采用分层（tiered）管线理念：以轻量级前馈重建（lightweight feed-forward reconstruction）支撑实时感知-跟踪闭环，而将重型神经重建按需调度至适合的计算节点上选择性执行。评估维度聚焦于渲染质量、GPU计算开销和推理/优化延迟之间的权衡。

Result: 主要实验发现包括：(1) 在同等条件下，3D Gaussian Splatting获得比NeRF更高的渲染质量，但代价是更大的GPU计算开销；(2) 在单板计算机等机载计算平台上，算力不足以支撑逐场景优化（per-scene optimization）达到交互速率（interactive rates），即完整逐场景最优化的实时性在边缘设备上不可行；(3) 对SAM3D的初步评估表明，它能以秒级延迟输出合理的对象几何结构，但存在细节失配（detail mismatches），这些细节误差可能损害下游操作（downstream manipulation）的可靠性。实验设置涵盖多种GPU设备（单板机至服务器节点），评价维度包括训练/渲染性能、延迟与重建保真度。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献在于首次对神经3D重建方法进行跨计算平台（从边缘到服务器）的系统性实时性基准评测，为实验室机器人部署提供了可量化的算力-质量权衡依据。其主要技术启示是：单一重建策略难以同时满足实时性与质量需求，应构建分层管线——轻量前馈网络承担实时感知跟踪任务，重型逐场景优化在算力充足的节点按需调度。局限方面，SAM3D的评估仍属"初步"（preliminary），其对下游操作影响的量化尚不完整；且研究聚焦于计算性能，未深入涉及构建级精度的物理意义或不同机器人操作场景下的差异化需求。

Abstract: Autonomous robots performing laboratory tasks depend on 3D reconstruction pipelines that can turn raw camera streams into actionable object representations within the latency budget of a physical control loop. Neural 3D reconstruction methods have demonstrated high-quality view synthesis, but their real-time viability across the compute platforms on which laboratory robots actually run remains poorly characterized. In this work, we present a systematic compute-platform benchmark of neural 3D reconstruction methods, evaluating NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting training and rendering on GPU-enabled computing devices ranging from single-board computers to server-class nodes, and place Meta's SAM3D single-image reconstruction on the same axes to quantify its latency and fidelity gap relative to per-scene optimization. Our results show that Gaussian Splatting yields higher rendering quality than NeRF at greater GPU cost, and that onboard compute is insufficient for full per-scene optimization at interactive rates. Our preliminary assessment on SAM3D indicates that it delivers plausible object geometry within seconds, but with detail mismatches that can compromise downstream manipulation. Together, these findings motivate tiered pipelines in which lightweight feed-forward reconstruction sustains the real-time perception-and-tracking loop for laboratory robots, while heavier neural reconstruction is scheduled selectively on suitable compute.

</details>


### [177] [RTNav: Towards Real-Time Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26496)
*Easop Lee,Lingyu Zhang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RTNav是一种面向真实时间约束的零样本目标导航架构，将推理延迟、异步环境步进和有界计算资源作为显式设计考量。在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON的真实时间变体评估中，相比现有方法将成功率提升最多11%，按完成时间加权的成功率（SCT）提升最多5.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法大多依赖视觉-语言基础模型进行感知和推理，但这些模型的推理延迟不可忽略。绝大多数SOTA方法在同步模拟器中开发，环境等待智能体动作、推理时间被视作免费，因此智能体架构围绕"感知→推理→动作"的串行执行设计，几乎不考虑时间约束。在真实世界墙钟时间计入任务预算的情况下，这些低效架构会遭受严重性能退化。论文证明近期零样本导航方法在这种真实时序条件下出现一致的性能下降，从而提出将推理延迟显式纳入设计。

Method: RTNav提出一种简洁而有效的架构，将三个关键要素作为显式设计原则：(1) 推理延迟——将基础模型的推理耗时视为真实约束，而非假设其零成本；(2) 异步环境步进——允许智能体与环境的交互不必严格串行，从而在感知推理进行中利用空闲时间；(3) 有界计算资源——在有限算力预算内做决策。架构的核心创新在于打破传统"串行感知-推理-动作"管线，通过异步化与环境交互、在推理延迟期间继续收集信息等方式减少墙钟时间浪费，使智能体在真实时间约束下做出更高效的导航决策。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON三个基准数据集的真实时间变体上进行评估，这些变体将真实推理延迟与墙钟时间计入任务预算。实验结果显示，RTNav相比现有最先进零样本导航方法：成功率（Success Rate）提升最高达11%；按完成时间加权的成功率（SCT，Success weighted by Completion Time）提升最高达5.1个百分点。该结果表明，在引入真实时间约束后，传统串行架构的性能退化可被RTNav有效缓解。

Conclusion: RTNav的主要贡献在于揭示了一个长期被忽视的问题：同步模拟器中的零样本导航方法在真实时间约束下的性能退化，并提出了一种以推理延迟、异步步进和有界计算为显式设计考量的架构来应对。其意义在于推动agent设计从"同步模拟优先"转向"实时执行优先"的范式。潜在局限性包括：论文主要在仿真（尽管是真实时间变体）中评估，"真实世界"泛化仍需实际机器人验证；且对具体异构计算调度或硬件平台的适配细节未充分展开。该工作与事件相机/SNN等极限低延迟感知的关联在于，它从系统层面论证了感知延迟对实时任务目标的影响，与事件驱动感知追求的低时延优势目标一致。

Abstract: Navigation in unknown environments to find unforeseen objects has become increasingly feasible with capable vision and language foundation models. However, these models also introduce non-negligible inference latency, which becomes an important concern when agents must operate continuously in the real world. Most state-of-the-art methods are still developed in synchronous simulators, where the environment waits for the agent to act and inference time is effectively free. As a result, agents are often designed around the sequential execution of perception, reasoning, and action, with little regard for time constraints. Under real-time execution, where wall-clock time counts towards the task budget, the inefficiencies of these architectures become clear. We show that recent zero-shot object navigation methods suffer consistent performance degradation under such realistic timing conditions. Motivated by this observation, we propose RTNav, a simple but effective architecture that treats inference latency, asynchronous environment stepping, and bounded compute as explicit design considerations. Evaluated on real-time variants of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, RTNav improves the success rate by up to 11% and the Success weighted by Completion Time by up to 5.1 points over prior work.

</details>


### [178] [Closing the Loop on the Poppy Humanoid: Bipedal Locomotion with Linear-Quadratic Control and Learned Cost Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26505)
*Xulin Chen,Borui He,Ruipeng Liu,Naveed Tahir,Zhenyu Gan,Garrett E. Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性二次调节器（LQR）框架的闭环双足行走控制器，用于开源低成本人形机器人Poppy Humanoid。该方法利用开环回放标称行走轨迹采集的数据，学习LQR控制器的二次代价函数，从而显著提升运动可靠性，并首次实现在标准Poppy硬件上的可靠、自主双足步行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poppy Humanoid是一款开源、低成本、适用于AI研究与教育的人形机器人，但此前没有公开的方法能在标准硬件上实现可靠、无需人工协助的双足运动。双足行走本质上是不稳定且高度非线性的动态问题，开环轨迹回放极易因扰动和建模误差而失稳。该问题的重要性在于：可靠的自主双足运动是机器人学和人形智能研究的基础，缺乏可行的控制方法会严重限制Poppy作为教育与科研平台的实用价值。

Method: 该方法围绕LQR框架构建闭环行走控制器，核心思路是：先以标称行走轨迹进行开环回放并采集系统状态数据，再利用这些数据学习一个二次代价函数（cost function），使得LQR控制器在该代价下能进行有效的轨迹跟踪。这种从数据中学习代价函数的方式，避免了手动设计复杂的加权矩阵，让控制器能够针对Poppy的实际动力学特性进行优化。LQR作为线性最优控制框架，通过状态反馈实现对标称轨迹的闭环跟踪，从而抵消扰动和模型误差。

Result: 闭环控制器在标准Poppy硬件上进行了实证验证。实验对比了所提闭环LQR控制器与开环轨迹回放在步行性能上的差异，结果表明闭环控制器在行走性能上实现了统计学显著的提升（statistically significant improvements），验证了数据驱动的代价学习与LQR框架相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献在于：首次为标准Poppy Humanoid硬件提供了被实证验证的可靠双足行走方法，展示了从开环数据中学习LQR代价函数这一实用且可复现的技术路线。该方法成本低、基于开源平台，具有较高的可访问性和推广价值。局限性可能包括：LQR作为线性控制器本质上依赖局部线性化，可能无法应对大幅扰动或高度动态的地形；行走的泛化性（不同速度、转向、地形）和鲁棒性边界有待进一步探索。

Abstract: The Poppy Humanoid is an open-source, low-cost robot suitable for research and education in artificial intelligence. However, we are unaware of any published methodology that achieves reliable, unassisted bipedal locomotion on the standard Poppy hardware. This paper contributes a functional closed-loop walking controller for Poppy, based on the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) framework for trajectory tracking. Starting with data collected from open-loop playback of a nominal walking trajectory, our proposed method learns a quadratic cost function for an LQR controller that substantially improves the reliability of the motion. The closed-loop controller is validated empirically, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in walking performance compared to open-loop trajectory playback.

</details>


### [179] [Memory Anchors for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26545)
*Maximilian Du,Zhanyi Sun,Chen Xu,Paarth Shah,Masha Itkina,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"Memory Anchors"概念，指出在机器人策略的持续学习（continual learning）中，回放缓冲区（replay buffer）中仅有一小部分过往经验对防止灾难性遗忘起关键作用。这些锚点位于新任务观测表征与旧任务表征坍缩但动作冲突的区域内。通过识别并保留这些锚点，能在LIBERO基准上将高冲突任务遗忘降低63%，排除10%锚点则导致遗忘增加4.5倍以上，并在真实机器人上实现了两个任务序列的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外部署的机器人策略需要持续学习新任务而不遗忘已有行为，这是典型的灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）问题。现有通用做法是使用包含过往任务数据的回放缓冲区进行重放训练，但缓冲区通常对所有历史经验进行随机均匀采样。本文指出随机采样忽视了经验在大脑中（网络表征空间中）分布的不均匀性——只有少量经验承担着"锚定"过往性能的关键作用。识别这些关键经验并对其进行有针对性重放，是提升持续学习效率、降低遗忘的关键。该问题对机器人自主适应、终身学习等实际部署场景具有重要意义。

Method: 本文提出"Memory Anchors"定义与识别方法。核心洞察是：在新任务观测的表征空间中，某些区域会与旧任务观测的表征发生坍缩/重合，但这些区域所对应的任务却要求相互冲突的动作（例如熟悉的物体需要以新方式被操作）。这类区域内的旧数据在重放时能有效防止过去任务知识的破坏性覆盖，因此被称为Memory Anchors。方法流程为：(1) 通过表征空间分析，识别新-旧任务表征坍缩且动作冲突的区域；(2) 在这些区域中挑选旧任务经验作为Memory Anchors；(3) 在构造回放缓冲区时对这些锚点进行优先加权或丰富化采样，替代原有的纯随机均匀采样策略。

Result: 实验在LIBERO基准套件上完成。结果显示：仅在采样缓冲区前排除10%的Memory Anchors，灾难性遗忘就会增加超过4.5倍，证明这些锚点的稀缺性与关键性。反之，在回放缓冲区中丰富Memory Anchors后，高冲突任务的遗忘降低了63%。此外，该方法在真实机器人上成功实现了两个任务序列的持续学习。论文还提供了可视化与视频演示。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：(1) 首次提出并形式化"Memory Anchors"概念，表明持续学习中回放经验的价值极不均匀；(2) 给出了基于表征坍缩与动作冲突区域的锚点识别机制；(3) 提出基于锚点富集的重放采样策略，在仿真基准和真实机器人上均显著降低灾难性遗忘。局限性可能包括：锚点识别依赖表征相似度与动作冲突度量，在新任务分布偏移较大或任务序列较长时，其识别稳定性与扩展性有待进一步验证；方法对表征质量的敏感性也需更多分析。

Abstract: Robot policies deployed in the wild should have the capability to continually learn new tasks without forgetting existing behaviors. A common approach to combat such catastrophic forgetting is to train on new task data with a replay buffer of previously learned task data. Although this buffer is commonly sampled randomly from all prior experiences, we show that a small set of these experiences contributes greatly in anchoring past performance. We call these experiences Memory Anchors. We identify Memory Anchors in regions where representations of new-task observations collapse onto those of old-task observations even though the tasks require conflicting actions, like when a familiar object must be manipulated in a new way. Rehearsing old data in this region plays a key role in preventing destructive overwriting of past task knowledge, serving as this critical Memory Anchor role. Excluding only 10% Memory Anchors before sampling the buffer leads to more than a 4.5x increase in catastrophic forgetting on the LIBERO benchmark suites. Conversely, enriching the replay buffer with Memory Anchors can decrease high-conflict task forgetting by 63% and enables successful continual learning of two task sequences on a real robot. Videos and additional visualizations can be found at https://robot-adaptation.github.io/MemoryAnchors

</details>


### [180] [TrapVLA: Trapping Vision-Language-Action Models in Configured Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26578)
*Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Xu-Han Chen,Yinghao Li,Zhuohao Li,Yuan-Ming Li,Qing Zhang,Xiaoyi Fan,Dongmei Jiang,Yan Li,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"Configured Failure Trapping"（配置化失败陷阱），一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的全新后门攻击任务。与以往将任意任务失败视为攻击成功的后门攻击不同，该任务要求攻击者通过隐蔽的文本触发器控制机器人"如何失败"（例如按指定位置偏移抓取），显著提升了攻击的挑战性与隐蔽性。作者构建了数据引擎用于合成高质量目标轨迹，设计自动评估套件，并构造Trap-LIBERO和Trap-RoboTwin两个基准，涵盖四种失败模式。在此基础上提出TrapVLA方法，显式学习触发器诱导的动作残差以引导策略产生配置化失败行为。实验证明在模拟和真实机器人设置中，TrapVLA能有效注入配置化失败模式，同时基本保持干净数据上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLA模型的后门攻击存在根本性局限：它们仅关注"任务是否失败"，将任何偏离目标的动作都视为攻击成功。然而在实际规约中，攻击者往往需要精准控制机器人的失败方式（如特定抓取偏移），这种"配置化失败"对攻击能力提出了更高要求，同时也更难被系统检测。该问题的研究空白在于：(1)缺乏对配置化失败后门攻防的系统化定义和形式化度量；(2)缺少高质量的目标轨迹合成手段与评估工具；(3)已有后门注入方法难以学习稀疏、局部的动作偏差。该工作的重要性在于首次将VLA后门攻击从"二值化成功/失败"提升到"可控失败模式"层面，对安全敏感的具身智能和机器人部署场景（如家庭服务、无人机、自动驾驶）具有显著威胁评估与防御研究价值。

Method: 核心技术贡献包括三部分。第一，任务定义：形式化Define Configured Failure Trapping，攻击者指定一个触发条件（文本/视觉）和目标失败行为fθ，攻击目标是在保持干净输入下策略正常工作的前提下，仅在有触发器时定向诱导指定失败模式。第二，数据引擎：设计自动合成高质量目标轨迹的pipeline，通过对正常示范轨迹施加可控的失败变换（如抓取偏移、旋转误差等）生成带标签的目标轨迹，并配套自动度量套件来量化"配置化失败保真度"（configured-failure fidelity），包括失败模式是否精确匹配、动作偏差是否符合指定分布等指标。第三，TrapVLA方法：识别出关键难点在于"稀疏动作偏差"——配置化失败往往只体现为动作序列中极少数维度/时间步的微小偏移，难以被标准后门训练范式学习。TrapVLA显式建模触发器诱导的动作残差（action residual），将输出分解为正常策略动作与一个稀疏残差项之和，训练时仅对该残差施加强约束，从而在最小化对干净数据行为干扰的同时，精确注入配置化失败。模型架构基于VLA主干（策略头 + 残差分支），通过门控机制在触发条件下激活残差路径。

Result: 作者构建了两个基准：Trap-LIBERO（基于LIBERO仿真套件）和Trap-RoboTwin（基于RoboTwin），覆盖四种代表性失败模式（如位置偏移抓取、旋转偏差、运动轨迹偏差、最终位姿错误等）。评估指标包括：配置化失败触发成功率（triggered failure success rate）、失败行为与目标配置的保真度（配置化失败保真度）、以及干净数据上的任务成功率（衡量后门是否干扰正常性能）等。实验在仿真基准和真实机器人平台上进行，结果显示TrapVLA能有效注入配置化失败模式，且相比baseline（标准后门攻击方法）在失败保真度上有显著提升，同时基本保持干净数据上的成功率（退化很小）。具体数值涉及各类失败模式的成功注入率和保真度指标，表明方法在精确控制失败行为上优于现有后门范式。

Conclusion: 本文在安全与后门攻击研究领域做出了开拓性贡献：首次系统化定义了针对VLA模型的"配置化失败陷阱"任务，并配套构建了数据引擎、评估套件及两个公开基准（Trap-LIBERO、Trap-RoboTwin），为后续该方向的攻防研究提供了标准化评测平台。方法上，TrapVLA以"显式动作残差学习"解决了稀疏动作偏差的核心挑战，在不损害干净性能的前提下实现对失败行为的精细定向控制。局限性包括：当前方法主要面向文本触发器和仿真与部分真实场景，失败模式的泛化范围有限；配置化失败的定义依赖攻击者对动作空间的先验划分；同时论文未深入讨论对防御机制（如后门检测、I/O鲁棒性校验）的对抗鲁棒性；计算开销与VLA大模型的实际部署约束也值得后续研究。

Abstract: This work introduces Configured Failure Trapping, a novel backdoor attack task against Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, which aims to activate attacks through stealthy textual triggers and induce configured failure modes. Unlike prior backdoor attacks that treat any task failure as a successful attack, Configured Failure Trapping requires the attacker to control how the robot fails (e.g., causing the robot to grasp with a specified positional offset), making it substantially more challenging and hard to detect. To support the new task, we propose an effective data engine for synthesizing high-quality target trajectories and an automated suite for measuring configured-failure fidelity. Then, based on this foundation, we construct two new benchmarks, namely Trap-LIBERO and Trap-RoboTwin, that instantiate Configured Failure Trapping across four representative failure modes. To address this task, we identify sparse action deviation as a critical challenge and accordingly propose a novel method named TrapVLA, which explicitly learns trigger-induced action residuals to steer the policy toward the configured failure behavior. Extensive experiments across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic settings show that TrapVLA effectively injects configured failure modes into VLA models while largely preserving performance on clean data. Project page: https://john-liua.github.io/TrapVLA/

</details>


### [181] [SOLO: Stable Omni-terrain Long-Horizon Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26583)
*Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Jingkai Sun,Jiahao Ma,Zeran Su,Zelin Tao,Peiran Liu,Shuai Shi,Wei Cui,Zifan Wang,Jialin Yu,Wen Zhao,Kangning Yin,Jiaxu Wang,Jiahang Cao,Lingfeng Zhang,Hao Cheng,Jian Tang,Yijie Guo,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOLO是一个统一的框架，用于解决感知型人形机器人在长时程复杂地形行走中的脆弱性问题。它包含两个核心组件：Query Reconstructor (QR)用傅里叶编码的cell查询从深度-本体感受token中检索空间维度的证据，保留地形尖锐边界；Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation将教师-学生状态差异融入PPO奖励，通过广义优势估计(GAE)实现时间信用分配。在仿真中QR将高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍，TA-MSE超过PPO和MSE+PPO；在压力测试地形上SOLO达到97.5%平均穿越成功率和96%踏脚石成功率，并零样本部署在真实机器人上完成1.5公里户外路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能在复杂地形上长距离行走保持平衡，而感知型人形机器人策略会因感知与控制误差累积而变得脆弱。论文指出两个加剧长时程脆弱性的复合原因：(1) 稠密地形重建会平滑掉对行走至关重要的动作细节（如台阶边缘、踏脚石边界）；(2) 逐点(pointwise)模仿缺乏时间维度的信用分配，无法将未来的不利后果归因到更早的动作上。这两个问题共同导致人形机器人在长距离、复杂地形上的鲁棒性严重不足，亟需一个统一框架同时解决空间重建精度与时间信用分配问题。

Method: SOLO框架包含两大技术创新：(1) Query Reconstructor (QR)：使用傅里叶编码的cell查询，从深度-本体感受token中检索空间上特定(spatially specific)的证据，从而重建地形高度图并保留尖锐边界信息，避免稠密重建导致的细节平滑化。(2) Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation：在PPO奖励中加入"下一步教师-学生分歧"(next-state teacher-student disagreement)作为额外奖励信号，使GAE能将未来的分歧惩罚反向传播到之前的动作上，实现时间维度的信用分配，弥补逐点MSE模仿策略缺乏时序归因的缺陷。二者结合：QR解决空间重建精度，TA-MSE解决时间信用分配，联合解决长时程脆弱性。

Result: 在仿真实验中，QR将高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍；TA-MSE在课程进度(curriculum progression)上优于纯PPO和MSE+PPO组合。在压力测试地形(stress-test terrains)上，SOLO达到97.5%的平均穿越成功率和96%的踏脚石成功概率，而使用稠密重建器变体的方案仅有75.0-75.6%的平均成功率和0-3%的踏脚石成功率，体现了稠密重建的显著性能退化。真实部署方面，SOLO零样本(zero-shot)仅使用胸部安装的深度相机和本体感受，就完成了连续1.5公里的户外路线和室内混合地形课程，验证了跨仿真-真实的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SOLO的贡献在于提出一种同时解决"空间重建细节丢失"和"时间信用分配缺失"两大问题的统一框架。QR的傅里叶编码cell查询机制证明了保留地形尖锐边界对长时程行走鲁棒性的关键作用；TA-MSE将教师-学生分歧纳入PPO奖励并利用GAE实现时序归因，是对点级模仿局限的有效补充。显著优势在于：仅用胸口深度相机+本体感受即可零样本部署完成实际长距离行走任务，工程落地性强。可能的局限包括：验证主要依赖仿真和特定户外/室内路线，泛化到更复杂多变地形、不同机器人本体，以及对感知噪声和SLAM漂移在更长距离下的鲁棒性仍需进一步考察。

Abstract: Humans traverse complex terrain over long distances without losing balance, whereas perceptive humanoid policies become fragile as perception and control errors accumulate. We present SOLO, a unified framework addressing two compounding causes of this long-horizon fragility: dense terrain reconstruction smooths action-critical details, and pointwise imitation lacks temporal credit assignment. Its Query Reconstructor (QR) uses Fourier-encoded cell queries to retrieve spatially specific evidence from depth-proprioception tokens, preserving sharp terrain boundaries. Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation adds next-state teacher-student disagreement to the PPO reward, enabling Generalized Advantage Estimation to propagate future disagreement penalties to preceding actions. In simulation, QR reduces height-map L1 error by factors of 3.3-4.0, while TA-MSE surpasses PPO and MSE+PPO in curriculum progression. On stress-test terrains, SOLO achieves 97.5% mean traversal success and 96% stepping-stone success, versus 75.0-75.6% and 0-3% for dense-reconstructor variants. Deployed zero-shot with only a chest-mounted depth camera and proprioception, SOLO completes a continuous 1.5-km outdoor route and an indoor mixed-terrain course. Project page: https://sunpihai-up.github.io/solo/

</details>


### [182] [Relaxation-Aware Multimodal Sensing of Soft Gripper Driven by Structure-Perception-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26622)
*Yanzhe Wang,Hao Wu,Ziyi Zheng,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"结构—感知—学习"一体化的软体机器人手稳定夹持框架。通过可变刚度软体夹爪结合机载视觉与红外热成像，实时跟踪形变与温度场；并基于温度耦合的粘弹性力表示与物理信息学习模型，对持握过程中因应力松弛导致的夹持力衰减进行显式补偿。在280秒力控夹持任务中，该方法以0.066N的均值绝对误差维持期望夹持力，相比固定孔径与仅瞬时补偿基线分别提升80%和95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人手虽然能提供安全、自适应的接触，但其聚合物材料的固有粘弹性会导致持握过程中应力松弛和夹持力持续衰减，难以实现长时间稳定持握。人类抓握通过阶段相关的刚度调节与持续感知反馈相结合来应对这一问题。本文旨在解决软体夹持中"力松弛导致力衰减"这一核心难题，将主动刚度调节（机制设计）与连续感知和学习补偿（AI）相结合，为软体机器人的可靠、持续抓握提供解决方案。

Method: 方法包含三个层面：(1) 结构层面：设计可变刚度软体夹爪，通过刚度调节实现阶段相关的刚度调控以缓解早期松弛。(2) 感知层面：采用机载视觉和红外热成像，实时跟踪夹爪的形变和温度场，实现交互状态的连续、非侵入式跟踪。(3) 学习层面：提出温度耦合的粘弹性力表示，将温度作为影响聚合物粘弹性行为的关键变量纳入力建模；配合物理信息学习模型（physics-informed learning），重建力的变化趋势并在持握期间提供显式力补偿。整体采用"机制—AI协同设计"思想：机制（可变刚度结构）决定可行交互区间，学习模型补偿粘弹性动态中剩余的不确定性。

Result: 实验采用280秒力控"抓取—持握"任务（force-controlled grasp-and-hold），以均值绝对误差（MAE）为评估指标。结果表明：所提方法能以0.066N的MAE维持期望夹持力，相比固定孔径（fixed-aperture）基线和仅瞬时补偿（instantaneous-only）基线分别获得约80%和95%的性能提升，验证了温度耦合建模与显式补偿对粘弹性力衰减抑制的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：(1) 提出结构—感知—学习一体化框架，将可变刚度机械设计与连续感知、学习补偿统一起来；(2) 通过温度场与视觉形变的联合监测，首次将温度作为粘弹性力补偿的关键信号；(3) 证明"机制—AI协同设计"范式在软体抓握中的有效性，机制塑造可行交互，学习补偿粘弹性不确定性。局限在于实验规模相对单一（280s任务），尚未充分讨论不同负载、不同环境温度与不同软体材料下的泛化能力，以及红外热成像在长期运行中的标定漂移等问题。

Abstract: Achieving stable, sustained grasping with soft robotic hands remains a fundamental challenge. Compliance enables safe and adaptive contact, yet the intrinsic viscoelasticity of soft polymers leads to stress relaxation and a continuous decay of grasping force during holding. Inspired by human grasping, which combines phase-dependent stiffness regulation with continuous sensing and feedback, this paper presents an integrated structure--perception--learning framework. We develop a variable-stiffness soft gripper that uses onboard vision and infrared thermography to track deformation and the temperature field in real time, preserving continuous tracking of the interaction state. To mitigate relaxation-induced force decay, we propose a temperature-coupled viscoelastic force representation, together with a physics-informed learning model, to reconstruct the force trend and provide explicit compensation during holding. Experiments show that, in a 280s force-controlled grasp-and-hold task, the proposed method maintains the desired force with a mean absolute error of 0.066N, outperforming fixed-aperture and instantaneous-only baselines by 80% and 95%, respectively. Overall, the results support a mechanism--AI co-design view: mechanisms shape feasible interactions, while learning compensates remaining uncertainty in viscoelastic dynamics, together enabling stable, sustained grasping.

</details>


### [183] [FLARE: A Failure-Aware Framework for Autonomous Correction and Recovery in Visual-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26645)
*Ganlong Zhao,Zijia Tang,Xingping Chen,Zhanghui Kuang,Ye Tian,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 FLARE 框架，通过"Retry"（重试）和"Reset"（重置）双重机制为视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）赋予鲁棒的错误恢复能力。在训练阶段引入扰动和桥接片段（decouple robot pose from environment state）使策略自动处理执行偏差；利用 MLLM 进行离线失败分析以识别 OOD 状态，并针对性收集少量物体中心化的"Reset"技能库；推理时由在线 MLLM 监视器仲裁任务执行与重置技能之间切换。实验表明该方法在接触密集、挑战性的机器人操作任务上显著提升了成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在复杂的长时序机器人操作任务中展现出良好的泛化能力，但其性能往往比较"脆弱"（brittle）。这是因为它们通常只在轨迹单调（trajectory-monotonic）、无失败的演示数据上训练。这种对"完美"数据的依赖使得它们无法从常见执行错误中恢复，例如抓取失败、物体掉落或意外碰撞。这些偏差和失败在现实机器人操作中不可避免，因此赋予 VLA 错误恢复能力是提升其实际部署鲁棒性的关键问题。

Method: FLARE 框架采用"Retry"和"Reset"的双阶段范式：

(1) Retry 机制：在演示数据中注入扰动（perturbation）和桥接片段（bridging segments），这些片段将机器人位姿（robot pose）与环境状态（environment state）解耦，从而让策略学习自主处理执行偏差，使策略能够从局部偏离的状态恢复到任务可行的轨迹。

(2) Reset 管道：针对关键的、导致状态破坏（state-breaking）的分布外（OOD）失败，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对执行视频进行离线失败分析，自动识别 OOD 状态。基于此分析结果，进行高效、有针对性的少量数据收集，训练一个物体中心化（object-centric）的"Reset"技能库，用于将环境恢复到任务有效状态。

推理阶段：完整框架集成上述学习的策略，由一个在线 MLLM 监视器（monitor）仲裁，在任务执行和"Reset"技能之间动态切换。

Result: 论文在多种具有挑战性、接触密集（contact-rich）的机器人操作任务上进行了实验评估。结果表明，与基线方法相比，FLARE 框架显著提升了任务成功率（task success）和鲁棒性（robustness）。实验验证了 Retry 机制（通过扰动注入和桥接片段提升对执行偏差的鲁棒性）和 Reset 管道（通过 MLLM 驱动的 OOD 检测和特定技能训练实现状态恢复）两部分的有效性，证明了两者整体集成的价值。论文未在摘要中提供具体数值指标的详细列表。

Conclusion: FLARE 的核心贡献在于：(1) 提出了"Retry"机制，通过在训练数据中注入扰动和桥接片段来解耦机器人位姿与环境状态，从而提升 VLA 对执行偏差的自主恢复能力；(2) 提出了"Reset"管道，利用 MLLM 进行离线失败分析与在线监视仲裁，实现针对 OOD 失败的状态恢复技能学习；(3) 整体集成框架显著提升了接触密集操作任务的鲁棒性。该方法为"完美数据依赖"的 VLA 训练范式提供了一条新的改进路径。可能的局限性包括：对在线 MLLM 监视器的依赖可能引入推理开销和延迟；Reset 技能库的覆盖范围可能受限于离线失败分析中识别到的 OOD 状态类型；实验主要针对接触密集操作任务，其在更广泛场景（如事件相机感知、无人机视觉等）的适用性尚未探讨。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models~(VLAs) have demonstrated significant promise in generalizing to complex, long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks. However, their performance remains brittle, as they are typically trained on trajectory-monotonic, failure-free demonstrations. This reliance on ``perfect" data leaves them unable to recover from common execution errors, such as a missed grasp, a dropped object, or an unexpected collision. In this paper, we propose FLARE, a novel framework that endows VLAs with robust error recovery capabilities through a ``Retry" and ``Reset" paradigm. First, we introduce a ``Retry" mechanism by injecting perturbation and bridging segments that decouple robot pose from environment state into demonstrations, enabling the policy to autonomously handle execution deviations. Second, to address critical, state-breaking (OOD) failures, we introduce a ``Reset" pipeline. We leverage an MLLM for offline failure analysis to automatically identify OOD states from execution videos. This analysis enables the efficient, targeted collection of a small library of object-centric ``Reset" skills, which are trained to restore the environment to a task-valid state. Our full framework integrates these learned policies. At inference, an online MLLM monitor arbitrates between task execution and ``Reset" skills. Experiments on challenging, contact-rich manipulation tasks show our approach significantly improves task success and robustness.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond the Proving Ground: Independent Public-Road Testing of Assisted Lane Change Systems using LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26669)
*Marcello Cellina,Akos Kriston,Antonio Migneco,Davide Maggi,Stefano Favelli,Fabrizio Re,Fabrizio Minarini,Andrea Nuovo,Riccardo Dona,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在公共道路上独立测试商用高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）辅助变道功能的方法，并在法国A31高速公路上开展实车测试。利用基于LiDAR的车辆检测与跟踪系统，结合实时动态GNSS接收机，对比了记录的变道轨迹与UNECE第79号法规中的变道抑制要求。在27个预定义变道机动中，18个完成、9个被抑制，其中6次系统允许了不符合法规最小距离要求的机动，3次在考虑测量不确定性后偏差仍具统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文针对ADAS安全验证的核心痛点：试验场场景无法反映真实道路驾驶的复杂性，而地理围栏（geo fencing）测试则需要制造商合作且会限制评估独立性。辅助变道系统（Assisted Lane Change）作为L2级驾驶辅助功能，其类型批准（type approval）和在役（in-service）合规性测试缺乏独立的公共道路评估方法。法规合规测试（特别是UNECE R79对变道安全距离的要求）需要独立、真实环境下的验证手段，这对市场监督和法规修订具有重要意义。

Method: 作者提出一种独立的公共道路测试方法，核心创新包括：(1) 采用LiDAR为本的车辆检测与跟踪系统作为感知基准（ground truth），避免依赖被测系统的内部数据或制造商合作；(2) 利用实时动态运动学（RTK）GNSS接收机评估检测与跟踪精度；(3) 实测覆盖测试车与切入车辆（take over vehicle）之间的多种车间距与速度组合；(4) 将记录的真实变道轨迹与UNECE R79的变道抑制要求进行定量对比，评估系统是否符合法规规定的最小安全距离条件。

Result: 测试在法国A31高速公路上开展。设置27个预定义变道机动，结果为18个完成、9个被系统抑制。合规性分析发现：6例中系统允许了不满足法规最小距离要求的变道机动；其中3例在计入测量不确定性后，偏差仍具有统计显著性。结果表明LiDAR感知系统对这类合规评估具有充分适用性，且揭示了类型批准程序未覆盖的真实道路行为差异。

Conclusion: 该论文首次开展了针对辅助变道功能符合UNECE R79安全距离要求的公共道路测试，验证了基于LiDAR的独立测量方法论的有效性。其意义在于：(1) 提供了一种不依赖制造商的独立市场监督手段；(2) 揭示了真实道路环境下ADAS行为与法规要求之间的偏差，为法规修订提供实证依据。局限包括：测试限于单一高速公路（A31）、单一测试车辆、LiDAR系统的自身测量误差可能影响判定结果的确定性，以及样本量（27机动）相对有限。

Abstract: Testing of commercial Advanced Driver Assistance Systems is essential to ensure safety and compliance during type approval and in service operation. However, proving ground scenarios may not reflect real world driving complexity, while geo fencing can require manufacturer collaboration and limit assessment independence. This work presents a methodology for independently testing Assisted Lane Change systems on public roads. A campaign on the A31 French motorway used a test vehicle equipped with a LiDAR based vehicle detection and tracking system. Tests covered combinations of inter vehicle distance and speed between the test vehicle and the take over vehicle. Real time kinematic global navigation satellite system receivers assessed detection and tracking performance. Recorded lane change trajectories were compared with the lane change suppression requirements of UNECE Regulation Number 79. Of 27 predefined lane change manoeuvres, 18 were completed and 9 suppressed. In 6 cases, the system allowed manoeuvres that did not meet regulatory minimum distance requirements. In 3 cases, the deviation remained statistically significant after accounting for measurement uncertainty. To the authors knowledge, this is the first public road campaign designed to assess Assisted Lane Change compliance with Regulation Number 79 safety distance requirements. The results demonstrate the suitability of LiDAR based sensing for this purpose. The methodology can support market surveillance and future regulatory revisions by revealing real world behaviours not covered by approval procedures.

</details>


### [185] [PredVLA: A Sub-Million-Parameter Predictive-Coding Policy for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26673)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredVLA是一种仅含68万可训练参数、无需机器人数据预训练的语言条件预测编码策略。其核心思想是采用层次化生成式循环动力学，通过预测视觉特征与本体感觉，并仅利用感官预测误差的在线推断来影响隐状态。在LIBERO基准上达到86.9%（短时数套件）和75.4%（含长时数套件）的平均成功率，在受控对比中分别达到参数匹配Transformer和LSTM策略成功率的3.7倍和7.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作基准上表现优异，但论文质疑：多少模型规模才是语言条件控制所真正必需的？是否能在极小的参数预算下使用根本不同的控制架构保持竞争力？这涉及机器人操作领域中模型规模化与架构设计之间的核心权衡问题，关乎部署效率、可解释性与实时性。

Method: PredVLA采用预测编码（predictive coding）框架：其层次化生成式循环动力学同时预测视觉特征与本体感觉，而观测仅通过"由预测误差驱动的隐状态在线推断"这一途径影响潜状态。这一表述使得"观测驱动的修正"贡献变得可直接测量——因为移除该推断机制即可得到精确的开环控制条件。架构参数仅0.68M，且不含机器人数据预训练，属于循环生成策略范式，区别于大规模Transformer VLA模型。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，PredVLA在三个短时数套件（short-horizon）中达到86.9%平均成功率，包含长时数套件后为75.4%。在受控对比（相同冻结前端、演示数据、动作解码器与评估协议）下，其平均成功率为参数匹配的Transformer和LSTM策略的3.7倍和7.4倍。通过禁用预测误差驱动的推断可实现精确开环控制，用于定量衡量观测修正的贡献。

Conclusion: PredVLA证明了"亚百万参数"的循环生成策略即可在现代语言条件操作基准上取得强性能，挑战了大规模VLA模型必要性的假设，并为预测误差驱动的在线状态修正提供了显式机制。其主要贡献在于：证明了极小参数量下架构设计的竞争力、预测编码框架的可解释性（可量化观测修正贡献）以及无需机器人预训练数据的能力。可能的局限包括：仅在LIBERO单一基准评估，且语言条件复杂任务上的泛化能力尚需更多验证。

Abstract: Large pretrained vision-language-action models dominate modern robot-manipulation benchmarks, but it remains unclear how much model scale is necessary for strong language-conditioned control, or whether fundamentally different control architectures can remain competitive at much smaller parameter budgets. We present PredVLA, a language-conditioned predictive-coding policy with only 0.68 million trainable network parameters and no robot-data pretraining, whose hierarchical generative recurrent dynamics predict visual features and proprioception while observations influence latent state only through online inference from the resulting sensory prediction errors. On LIBERO, PredVLA achieves an 86.9% mean success rate across the three short-horizon suites and 75.4% when the long-horizon suite is included. Under a controlled comparison using the same frozen front end, demonstrations, action decoder, and evaluation protocol, PredVLA achieves 3.7x and 7.4x mean success rates of parameter-matched Transformer and LSTM policies, respectively. The predictive-coding formulation also makes the contribution of observation-driven correction directly measurable: because observations influence the recurrent state only through prediction-error-based latent inference, disabling this inference yields an exact open-loop control condition. Together, these results show that a sub-million-parameter recurrent generative policy can achieve strong performance on modern language-conditioned manipulation benchmarks while providing an explicit mechanism for prediction-error-driven online state correction.

</details>


### [186] [Residual Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based Computed Torque Control for a Cable-Driven Lower-Limb Rehabilitation Robot under Disturbances and Parametric Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26739)
*Mohammad-Hossein Fakouri,Ali Keymasi-Khalaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制（CTC）框架，用于绳驱下肢康复机器人轨迹跟踪。计算力矩控制生成名义控制指令，一个有界的Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient（DDPG）策略仅提供额外的补偿力矩，在保持模型可解释结构的同时提升跟踪性能与抗扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳驱下肢康复机器人轨迹跟踪面临多重挑战：模型不确定性、外部扰动、关节约束以及只能拉拽（pull-only）的绳索驱动方式，都会使名义控制性能退化。传统基于模型的控制（如计算力矩控制）结构可解释，但对模型失配敏感；而纯学习式控制虽能补偿非线性，却会降低透明性并使约束感知操作复杂化。因此亟需一种既能提升鲁棒性、又保持模型可解释性的混合控制策略。

Method: 提出残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制（residual DRL-enhanced CTC）框架：1）计算力矩控制（CTC）作为基础控制器生成名义力矩指令；2）一个输出有界的DDPG策略学习残差补偿力矩，仅叠加在名义指令之上；3）通过有界策略输出保证补偿力矩在安全范围，从而满足关节极限与绳索拉力等约束。整体结构保持模型基命令的可解释性，同时利用强化学习补偿模型误差与外部扰动。

Result: 在仿真环境中评估，设置名义、不确定、扰动、组合及泛化多种条件，并进行轨迹跟踪、关节极限、绳索拉力需求、工作空间可行性和绳雅可比（cable-Jacobian）诊断等测试。结果表明：残差控制器相比纯计算力矩控制，在跟踪精度和扰动抑制上均有提升，同时保留了可解释的模型基指令结构，并在代表性评估中满足可行性检查。更广泛的泛化测试表明跟踪改善可以超出代表性场景持续存在，但也暴露出与轨迹相关的约束局限。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是将有界残差学习与模型基计算力矩控制结合，作为康复机器人控制的实用鲁棒性增强策略：无需完全替换控制器即可获得强化学习的补偿能力，同时保持控制透明度和约束可行性。局限性在于验证基于仿真，且约束满足具有轨迹依赖性，需进一步进行约束感知优化和真实机器人实验验证。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory tracking in cable-driven lower-limb rehabilitation robots is challenging because model uncertainty, external disturbances, joint constraints, and pull-only cable actuation can degrade nominal control performance. Conventional model-based controllers provide an interpretable control structure but remain sensitive to model mismatch, whereas fully learning-based control can reduce transparency and complicate constraint-aware operation. This study proposes a residual deep reinforcement learning-enhanced computed torque control framework in which computed torque control generates the nominal command and a bounded Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient policy supplies only an additional compensating torque. The approach is evaluated in simulation under nominal, uncertain, disturbed, combined, and generalization conditions, together with trajectory-tracking, joint-limit, cable-demand, workspace-feasibility, and cable-Jacobian diagnostics. Across the evaluated conditions, the residual controller improves tracking and disturbance rejection relative to computed torque control while preserving the interpretable model-based command structure and satisfying the reported feasibility checks in the representative evaluation. Broader tests indicate that tracking improvements can persist beyond the representative case while also exposing trajectory-dependent constraint limitations. These results support bounded residual learning as a practical robustness-enhancement strategy for simulation-based rehabilitation robot control and motivate further constraint-aware and experimental validation.

</details>


### [187] [MeshPriorDiT: Hierarchical Modeling for Action-Conditioned Cloth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26766)
*Zihang Wang,Jianming Hu,Shang Su,Hao Huang,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了 MeshPriorDiT，一种用于动作条件布料动力学预测的层次化模型。该模型将未来布料运动分解为"结构化网格先验 + 生成式残差"：动作条件网格 GNN 首先预测多步顶点位移生成参考轨迹，随后 Residual DiT 基于条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）在历史状态、规划动作与网格先验条件下生成网格 GNN 未捕获的残差运动，并通过材料邻接关系进行缩放与解码以协调相邻顶点的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布料动力学预测方法存在两个范式的根本缺陷：网格 GNN 虽能借助材料连通性捕获局部物理响应，但有限的消息传递范围制约了拓扑远距离区域间的协调，且自回归展开会累积预测误差；基于 Transformer 的动力学模型虽能通过全局注意力捕获长程交互，但通常缺乏显式的材料连通性，需直接从数据中学习局部拓扑响应。MeshPriorDiT 旨在融合两者的互补优势——利用 GNN 提供符合材料拓扑和抓取约束的结构化先验，同时借助 Diffusion Transformer 的生成能力补足先验缺失的残差运动。

Method: MeshPriorDiT 采用层次化两阶段架构：(1) 动作条件网格 GNN 预测多步顶点位移，生成尊重材料拓扑与抓取约束的参考轨迹（mesh prior）；(2) Residual DiT 以历史状态、规划动作和网格先验为条件，使用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）联合生成先验未能捕获的残差运动，采用流匹配范式替代传统扩散生成以提升采样效率；(3) 生成的残差通过材料邻接矩阵进行重缩放与解码，以协调相邻顶点间的修正动作。该设计将 GNN 的物理先验约束与 DiT 的全局生成能力紧密结合。

Result: 在三个布料操作任务上进行了 15 步自回归展开评估。跨任务平均结果显示：MeshPriorDiT 相比 GNN-Only 基线将平均全局 MSE（Global MSE）降低 43.42%，相比 DiT-DDPM 基线降低 75.03%；同时在 Edge-strain MSE 指标上保持了与 GNN-Only 相当的水平，说明其在显著提升全局坐标预测精度的同时，未牺牲局部材料应变（边应变）的物理合理性。实验结果表明层次化"先验+残差"分解有效缓解了自回归误差累积与全局协调不足的问题。

Conclusion: MeshPriorDiT 的核心贡献在于提出了"结构化网格先验 + 生成式残差"的层次化动力学预测框架，将物理连接性（GNN）与长程全局建模（DiT）有机结合，在三个布料操作任务上取得显著的精度提升。其主要局限可能包括：残差重缩放依赖材料邻接解码，对拓扑变化/切开情境的泛化能力有待验证；流匹配生成的推理成本较纯 GNN 更高，实时性需进一步评估；评估集中于 15 步扩展滚动，更长时间尺度的稳定性仍需验证。

Abstract: Action-conditioned cloth dynamics prediction requires both locally plausible deformation and long-range coordination. Existing approaches largely follow two paradigms. Mesh-based GNNs capture local physical responses through material connectivity. However, their finite message-passing range limits coordination between topologically distant regions, while autoregressive rollouts tend to accumulate prediction errors. Transformer-based dynamics models capture long-range interactions through global attention, but often operate without explicit material connectivity and must learn local topological responses directly from data.
